On the toy, thresholds fit at the line's edge on calibration missed it on test (2.3-3.4% present wrong). Next: a per-answer confidence learned on calibration documents, its threshold fit with a conformal risk bound (order 0029). Status: **OPEN -- far facts by address solved at the 450M (run 5: 0.946 exact, villages 0.953, text identical; order 0005); free-text keys reach unseen subjects on the toy (0006: 0.85-0.90 with seen phrasings); copy or refuse is the contract (0021, 0022): on the 450M it meets both wrong-answer lines (1.7% present wrong, 0.0% absent answered) at 32.9% refused. Next: a learned confidence to cut the refusals (0029), learn on a miss (0007), creativity (0008), capacity (0009).**

## 10. Prioritized validation order

**Campaign status (2026-07-23):** steps 1–6 below were executed the same day this order was written — see `VALIDATION_REPORT.md`. The list is kept with ✅/⏳ marks so the *next* session starts from what's actually left, not from a stale queue.

1. ✅ **H-B3** — done: `vsa_binding_head.py` reread; `UNBIND_ROLE` was misattributed (it's cubemind VM code; the head has no unbind at all). ✅ **H-B5** — done: grilly IS the shared substrate (cubemind's `BlockCodes` wraps it directly); all three algebra families live there.
2. ✅ **H-C6** — **DECIDED (user, 2026-07-23): hybrid** — fixed core (next: 128k–256k BPE on the proven pipeline) + retrieval head from day one + separately-gated dynamic tail later. ✅ **H-A1** — done: C_ctx=0 structural + 94% empirical forgetting measured on the real layer.
3. ✅ **H-A3 vs. H-A4** — done: the Zero-Forgetting Stability Benchmark exists (`validation/exp_a_forgetting.py`) and ran all four candidates. Headline: NLMS best capacity but oldest-first forgetting; SDM only order-agnostic method but lower capacity at matched state; flat-decay Hebbian worst measured. ✅ **NYT-dated iteration (H-G2) also done same day** (`validation/exp_g2_nyt_forgetting.py`): real correlated keys cost everyone ~2–3× recall but the ranking survives unchanged — it can be trusted.
4. ✅ **H0 / H-A2** — done at toy scale: supported ONLY in the hardened form (naive hypernetwork is *worse* than baseline); P5 clean on the hardened variant. ✅ **Learned-context follow-up (H0 part 2 + H-C4) also done** (`validation/exp_h0b_learned_context.py`): with context INFERRED by a router instead of given, an unsupervised router collapses to one slot (reverts to baseline) and even a task-supervised router trained *online* forgets its own routing (slots drift 6→3 distinct, task-0 confidence →37%). Finding: **context inference, not θ=f(c) generation, is the bottleneck** — the router must be an OFFLINE-pretrained classifier (H-C4's `domain_head.pt` is exactly that), not learned online. ⏳ Still open: the hardening cost curve vs number of protected contexts, and H0 wired into a real LM memory pathway.
5. ✅ **H-B1 vs. H-B2 vs. H-B5** — the user-directed 3-way prototype over grilly's actual families is DONE (`validation/exp_b5_grilly_3way.py`, D=10240 / k=80×l=128, V=32k). Duties matrix: **block codes win or tie every duty except raw bind speed** — exact unbind (1.000, vs HRR's approximate 0.711), 100% readout at 5% signal (binary 84%, HRR 86%), 100% at n=320 superposition, 96.3% trainability through per-block softmax, readout ≈ binary speed (37ms @32k). Binary is 60× faster at bind (3µs) and trains at 99.6% when tanh-relaxed — but the hard-sign STE path measures **19.8%**, turning block_ops.py's "zero-gradient problem of bipolar sign()" claim from assertion into measurement. HRR: approximate unbind, 11× slower readout, 12.8% on the probe (partly probe-artifact — untuned shared lr/temperature — but the two independent negatives stand). Cross-implementation parity verified (grilly BinaryOps.bind ≡ cubby-concepts bind). **DECIDED (user, 2026-07-23): standardize the binding head on `BlockCodeOps`** — NVSA sparse block codes, k=80×l=128, one algebra across model + world-arena (also cubemind's existing substrate; Vulkan path confirmed initializing on this machine's RX 6750 XT). BinaryOps/HolographicOps remain reference implementations, not the standard. **Two grilly hazards found while surveying it for reusable VSA work (2026-08-02).**

(a) **`blockcode_bind` exists twice with different semantics, and one silently corrupts bundles.** `grilly.experimental.vsa.block_ops.BlockCodeOps.bind` (what `cubbyllm.ops.BlockCodeVSA` actually calls) is true per-block circular convolution. `grilly.backend._bridge.blockcode_bind` — both the GPU shader `shaders/blockcode-bind.glsl` and its numpy fallback — is a one-hot **argmax shift**: it scans `if (a[i] > 0.5) hot_a = i`, so on a continuous block code (every entry < 0.5, since each block sums to 1 across l slots) both indices stay 0 and it returns the **identity element without raising**. CubbyLLM is safe only because its ops seam never dispatches to `_bridge` — see the TODO item, which now says *do not* wire that dispatch. grilly's own `tests/test_blockcode.py` cannot catch this because every test uses strictly one-hot inputs; there is no test anywhere in grilly that unbinds from a superposition. Ours now has three: `tests/ops/test_vsa.py`.

(b) **`blockCodeCodebook` caps at n <= l — 128 codes at l=128.** IBM's successive-binding construction walks the cyclic group Z_l per block with the same shift each step, so it wraps after exactly l entries; the C++ raises `n must be <= l`, and the Python twin *silently* degrades to random sampling "(may have collisions for large n)". Irrelevant for role/filler duty (n=16), but it means the decided k=80 x l=128 shape cannot supply a large concept codebook by that construction. Independent random one-hot per block gives ~l^k distinct codes with birthday-bounded collisions, at the cost of algebraic closure — budget that design choice before the VM needs a vocabulary-scale codebook.

Group B's remaining open item is now only the generalization question from H-B5 (SVC role-filler scheme → token/concept binding) plus building the real unbind on the VM side (H-B3's relocated conclusion). **Added 2026-07-30 (H-B6):** a *separate* differentiable path — a parameter-free MAP binding aux loss — now shapes the trunk to be VSA-bindable during LM pretraining, validated live at 2B (binding loss → ~0.20 plateau, bpc unharmed through full-LR onset out to step 3000). Mechanism VERIFIED; "free" still PLAUSIBLE pending a clean `ckpt_1` A/B and full-run confirmation; and MAP→`BlockCodeOps` transfer is untested (the aux loss uses MAP for differentiability; the production algebra is a different family).
6. ✅ **H-C7** — done ahead of H-C6 because it turned out decision-independent: direct readout scales to V=1M at D=10240; flat factored readout does not; memory/compute (H-C3) is the real constraint either way.
7. ⏳ **H-G3** — unchanged: dedup/NSFW-filter/reproducibility pass on `unified/` before any real training run.
8. ✅ **Group D backbone (H-D1) — RESOLVED: MinGRU.** The in-architecture bake-off (`exp_d1b_backbone_bakeoff.py`, 4 candidates in the real CubbyModel on subword TinyStories) found gated recurrence beats attention-free Q=K and softmax attention by ~10% bpc; MinGRU ties GRU at 25% fewer params + a parallel scan. Promoted to `cubbyllm/model/backbone/mingru.py` as the package default. H-D2's TTT compute side stays measured-but-quality-untested; a faithful BDH + TTT backbone at scale is a possible future revisit, not a blocker.
9. ⏸ **Group E (hardware)** — deferred by design (H-E3, now verified by execution: the whole campaign ran CPU-only).
10. ⏳ **H-G5 (later / SFT phase)** — synthetic domain-tagged Q&A distilled via OpenRouter from the domain-sorted corpus, once it's pinned (H-G3). Prototype the generator small now to lock format/quality; defer the bulk spend to after pretraining. Not part of the pretraining gate.
11. ✅ **Group P (added 2026-08-25)** — H-P1–H-P5 toy-scale VERIFIED and the H-P1/P2/P3/P4 checkpoint arms run the same day on `ab_hybrid.pt` / `hd5_mem2.pt` (`validation/prospection/`, logs linked per entry). Scale verdicts: forking exact and bounded (P1); the toy sparsity of choice points does NOT transfer — 70–90% of real-text steps above 1–3 nats, budget rule needs a relative criterion (P2); **the trained recurrence is a 1–2-step mixer, all long-range work is in the attention layers** (P3); interpolation-based keyframe compression is dead, recompute wins (P4). ✅ **H-P6 RUN 2026-08-26 (A100, 65M tokens/arm):** the multi-horizon head is free (Δ held-out CE +0.002 nats) but redundant — a 300-step linear probe on the *baseline* trunk already reads the 50- and 500-token future at cosine 0.33 / 0.41 vs trivial 0.03 / 0.08, the objective adds ~0.02, and the recurrent gate spectra of the two arms are identical (1.2–2.1 steps). **Decision: not in the 2B runbook; successor-feature readouts become post-training probes on the frozen trunk.** ✅ **H-P7 RAN 2026-08-26 (Colab A100, 65.5M tokens/arm; verdict recorded 2026-08-28 from `validation/logs/exp_p7_{baseline,chrono}.*`): KILLED on CE** — chrono init costs +0.444 nats held-out at matched tokens (threshold 0.01), the τ spectrum erodes ~4× but survives (5–10% of units ≥ 100 steps vs 0 for the baseline), and the beyond-window impulse response is non-zero but ~1e-3. The gate can hold a long time constant; nothing useful goes in it at this scale. **Group P's last open route to long *recurrent* time constants is closed: beyond-window memory lives in the attention window and the episodic store — H-P3 final.** Chrono is not a cycle-one arm. Needle-recall (protocol step 4) was not run on Colab and deliberately not run after — ~13 CPU-hours for a read both arms would fail at 65M tokens (see the entry). ⏳ Still open in Group P: the relative-entropy reformulation of the P2 budget rule (re-test on the 2B checkpoint). Not part of the pretraining gate; the remaining roadmap items named in §9b's preamble get entries only once each has a cheap experiment.
12. ✅ **H-A7 — the learning gate (added 2026-08-28; built + RAN 2026-08-30 on Colab, `validation/logs/exp_a7_learning_gate*.*`): the gate SEPARATES.** LR ladder on `hd5_mem21.pt`, 3.3M-token updates: a replay update is CE-neutral on every source at 3e-5 (mix +0.005) and 1e-5 (−0.002) while the code-only update is still rejected there with the mechanism named (3e-5: mix +0.059, 12/12 unseen sources, memorized `pretrain_ext` +0.208, trained source −0.019; 1e-5: mix +0.017, `pretrain_ext` +0.053); at 1e-4 replay drifts uniformly (+0.046) and restoring the Adam state makes it worse — LR is the lever. Null promotes everywhere. **Nightly LR = 3e-5.** Three of the six proposed terms cannot see a trunk delta (CoT precision, routing precision, NYT-vs-NLMS); refinements queued: per-source ε from the paired SE, and a `uniform_regression` vs `forgetting` naming term. Gate eval 37–50 s/A100. Not part of the pretraining gate. ✅ **Counterfactual-neighborhood harvest (same day, DONE):** `exp_m3_cot_pipeline.py` now writes `counterfactuals[]` for every verified chain — see the H-F2/H-B3 CoT entry's 2026-08-28 addendum for the run.
13. ⏳ **Group K (added 2026-10-20) — the research pause.** H-K8's law and chunk store measured the same day (`validation/exp_k8_holo.py`: gate 1 failed as written — the law marks the 0.68 point, 90% holds to half of it; gate 2 passed at the line, 0.90); H-K1 read the same day (`validation/exp_k1_map.py`: the map 2× TransE on one hop, 4.6× on relation discovery, both compose; gate 2 failed as pre-registered); H-K7 gate 1 read (`exp_k7_exposure.py`: 0.00 / 0.00 / 0.50 / 0.99 at 1 / 10 / 100 / 1,000 sightings); H-K10 gate 1 read (`exp_k10_hypertoken.py`: a fact shown once answered at 0.94 beyond the window through the loci token, 0.00 for text; the learned route 0.00). Next, cheapest first: H-K7 gate 1 (the exposure curve on our trunk, a CPU afternoon), H-K4's host side (`Scene` with dims = 1), H-K8 gate 3 (a `CubbyMemory` sibling on a synthetic recall task), then H-K2 and H-K4's trunk sides on the grown model's second round, H-K9 on BlockWorld traces, H-K5's rule generator; H-K10's route read the same evening (the learned route 0.00 → 0.82 when taught by the route loss; attention over the whole document 0.03; a mismatched address 0.00) and its shards built (`facts`, `spatial_here` + text controls): the 450M run on them (`CB_SPATIAL=1 CB_SPATIAL_QUERY=placed CB_ROUTE_LOSS=1`) is the next Colab job — H-S12 gates 1–2 and H-K10 gate 4 in one.

## 11. What this document does not claim

It does not claim the imported research (GCE, BDH, Sylph, CMP, ZeTT, zip2zip, Hypencoder, the trillion-parameter blueprint) is correct — these are external claims supplied by the user, not independently reproduced by anyone on this project, except where a hypothesis above is explicitly marked VERIFIED (the cubby-concepts `vsa/` test suite and the SDM/resonator numbers, both independently re-run this session).

It does not claim any of the four surveyed sibling folders (cubby-concepts, AURA_GENESIS, GrillCheese, Novel_GNN_Arch) contain a ready-to-ship solution. The single strongest finding across all four, repeated because it matters: no implemented, validated fix for Hebbian catastrophic forgetting exists anywhere in the group (H-A6). That's a real, useful negative result, not a gap in the survey.

It does not commit to AIMC/analog hardware, BDH, or any specific backbone swap. All of those remain open, explicitly gated behind the cheaper validations in Section 10.

~~It does not claim `grilly.experimental.vsa.ops` (H-B5) is the same mechanism as cubby-lm's binding head or cubemind's block-codes.~~ **Resolved 2026-07-23:** grilly IS confirmed as the shared substrate for cubemind's block-codes (direct wrapper, verified in source) and is the named hash-lineage for the binding head's cross-repo codebook sharing; what this document still does NOT claim is that grilly's SVC role-filler scheme generalizes to CubbyLLM's token/concept binding needs — that remains open (see H-B5's validation result).

It also does not claim any toy-scale validation result in this document transfers to CubbyLLM's real scale — every "toy-scale VERIFIED" tag above (H0, H-A2, H-A5, H-C1's kill-half) is a mechanism demonstration with its scale caveat stated inline, and `VALIDATION_REPORT.md` ends with a "what would change these conclusions" list that should be treated as part of each result.
